## Band F — Grades 11–12 (Secondary 2–3, branches)

DRAFT — not committed to any repo. Prepared for the LiqLearns courses from the
held files only. All mathematics content below is filed in English; the book's
own language is stated per work. Short quoted fragments are headings or single
worked-example lines only; everything else is summary in my own words.

### Header: what this band is, and what is owed

- Country: Egypt. Subject: Mathematics. Grades: 11 (Second Secondary) and 12
  (Third Secondary).
- Files in hand: 24. Distinct works: 9. Total PDF pages across the 24 files:
  3,225 (counted with pdfinfo; see the clustering table at the end).
- Publisher for every work, unless the book says otherwise: the Egyptian
  Ministry of Education (Book Sector), printed by Sakkara Publishing
  (the Sakkara logo/name appears on the French and Arabic front matter).
  The author team is the same across most of the band, headed by
  Mr. Kamal Younis Kabsha; per-work author lists are given below.
- How the band is organised (proved from the covers, not from filenames):
  Grade 11 splits by section. Science-section pupils take TWO books per term:
  Pure Mathematics and Applied Mathematics (called "Mathematics
  Applications" on the Applied covers). Arts-section (literary) pupils take
  ONE book per term: General Mathematics, which is an abridged version of
  the Pure book, not a separate syllabus. Grade 12 has NO term split in this
  set: each Grade 12 subject is a single full-year student book.
  Grade 12 covers checked do not print a section label; Pure and Applied
  Grade 12 are the science-track continuation (same author teams, calculus
  and mechanics content), and Statistics is a separate full-year subject
  book whose section is likewise not printed on its cover or front matter.
- OWED FILE — file owed, not faked, not read: grade-12/gov/egypt/
  Economics_Statistics_3sec.pdf — "Statistics and Economics Secondary 12
  Term 1" (Arabic). Its ministry blob URL 404s, its Telegram copy
  (message 2759 in @liqlearns_textbooks, sent 2026-10-13) cannot be fetched
  from this host, and no mirror of the exact 2026 file was found. Whether
  its statistics part duplicates the Statistics Sec3 books below, and what
  economics content it carries, is UNVERIFIED until the file lands. It is
  counted nowhere in this draft's totals.

### SET-VERIFICATION traps — resolved from the books themselves

1. Missing French Term 2 files: GENUINELY ABSENT, not misfiled. All three
   Grade 11 branches (Pure, Applied, General) have a French Term 1 file and
   no French Term 2 file anywhere in the set. The French files present are:
   Grade 11 Term 1 in all three branches, plus the three full-year
   Grade 12 books. No other file in the set contains French Term 2 content:
   the only concatenated/doubled file found is the Arabic General Term 1
   file (see defect D1), and its second copy repeats Term 1, not Term 2.
2. Relationship of the three branches: General Mathematics is the Arts
   section's abridged Pure Mathematics. Proof: General Term 1 carries the
   same four unit titles as Pure Term 1 but drops lessons (no "Some
   properties of functions", no inverse function, and a Limits unit cut
   from six lessons to three); General Term 2 carries the same
   Sequences/Permutations/Calculus material as Pure Term 2 minus the
   summation-notation lesson, minus derivatives of trigonometric
   functions, minus applications of derivatives, and with the whole
   Trigonometry unit dropped. Applied Mathematics is a different subject
   altogether (mechanics plus, in Term 1, solid geometry) and shares no
   units with Pure or General.
3. Term 1 vs Term 2 splits are real in Grade 11: Term 1 and Term 2 books
   have disjoint contents with no overlap (Pure: functions/limits/trig
   then sequences/combinatorics/calculus/trig; Applied: statics/geometry
   then dynamics; General: the abridged Pure sequence in the same order).
   Grade 12 has no term split: one volume per subject covering the whole
   year (Pure runs from binomial theorem to definite integrals; Applied
   runs from statics to work/energy/power; Statistics runs from
   correlation to confidence intervals).
4. Pagination rule: the claim "PDF page = printed page" does NOT hold
   generally in this band and was verified per book, never assumed.
   Verified on the English editions (cover, contents and last-page
   footers read): Pure Term 1, General Term 1, Applied Term 1, General
   Term 2, Pure Grade 12, Applied Grade 12 and Statistics Grade 12 all
   run printed = PDF page minus 4 at the end of the book (e.g. Pure
   Term 1: PDF p.148 = printed p.144; Statistics: PDF p.146 = printed
   p.142, the normal-distribution table appendix). Two exceptions run
   PDF page = printed page: Pure Term 2 English (contents PDF pp.4–5
   are printed pp.4–5, last PDF p.136 = printed p.136) and Applied
   Term 2 English (last PDF p.72 = printed p.72). All printed page
   numbers in the unit maps below are the books' own printed numbers.
   Arabic and French editions were spot-checked against the English
   contents; their pagination was not matched page-for-page and may
   differ by a few pages (see the per-work notes).
5. Clustering: parallel language editions of one book are filed as ONE
   work. Editions were matched by cover title, section, term, contents
   titles and lesson page numbers, not by filename. Small page-number
   shifts between editions are real (e.g. French Pure Term 1 puts the
   logarithm-function lesson on printed p.73 and the cosine rule on
   p.140, against English pp.72 and 136) and are layout differences,
   not different content.

### Method note (what was actually checked)

- Every one of the 24 files: %PDF magic confirmed, pdfinfo page count
  taken, cover read, contents read, first and last pages read.
- Books with a real text layer were read in full text: Pure Term 2
  (Arabic and English), Statistics Grade 12 (all three languages),
  Applied Grade 12 (English and French), General Term 1 French, and
  General Term 2 Arabic. The remaining books are image-only scans; for
  those, every unit was read through its contents, unit opener (aims and
  vocabulary) and sampled lesson pages across the unit, and the unit
  maps below give every lesson title with its printed start page.
  A literal page-by-page visual pass of every image-only page was not
  completed in this pass; the maps, negatives and defects below rest on
  the contents plus the sampled pages, and any place where a claim rests
  only on the contents is a contents claim, not a whole-book claim.
- Arabic and French books are filed in English below; lesson titles are
  my translations of the printed titles, checked against the parallel
  English edition where one exists.

### The lesson pattern (all nine works)

Every unit opens with an introduction, a list of expected outcomes ("By
the end of this unit, the student should be able to"), and a vocabulary
list. Every lesson teaches through worked examples of rising difficulty,
then "Try to solve" exercises, then end-of-lesson exercises; units close
with general exercises, and several lessons carry "Enrich",
"Creative thinking" and "Discover the error" boxes. Calculator and
GeoGebra use is directed throughout. There are no separate teacher
guides in this band — every file is a student book.

---

### Work 1 — Pure Mathematics, Grade 11, Term 1, Science section

- Title as printed: "Pure Mathematics — Second Form Secondary — Student
  Book — First term — Science Section".
- Files: Arabic 148 PDF pages, English 148, French 150.
- Authors (English cover): Mr. Kamal Younis Kabsha, Prof.Dr. Afaf Abo
  Elfotouh, Mr. Serafiem Elias Iskander, Mr. Magdy Abdelfatah Essafty,
  Mr. Osama Gaber Abdelhafez. Revision/modifications: Dr. Mohamed Mohy
  abd-Elsalam, Mr. Shrif Atef EL-borhamy. Scientific supervision:
  Mrs. Manal Azkool. Pedagogical supervision: Dr. Akram Hassan.
- Edition/year as printed: school year 2025–2026 on all three covers.
  The English held copy has no imprint block on its inside cover (p.2
  is blank); its first-edition year, deposit number and ISBN could NOT
  be verified from the held English copy and are NOT assigned here
  (see record note R4 — the French copy's imprint block is misprinted
  with the Term 2 numbers and must not be copied onto Term 1).
- Pagination (English, verified): printed pp.2–144; printed = PDF − 4.
  French contents pages run a few pages later (see trap 5).
- Unit map (printed pages, English contents):
  - Unit One: Functions of a real variable and drawing curves.
    1-1 The real functions, p.4: domain, co-domain and range, and the
    function as a relation between two sets, with algebraic examples.
    1-2 Some properties of functions, p.13: one-to-one functions,
    symmetry about the y-axis and about the origin, and function
    composition. 1-3 Monotony of function, p.21: increasing, decreasing
    and constant behaviour over intervals. 1-4 Graphical representation
    of functions, p.26: drawing quadratic, modulus, cubic and rational
    curves and reading their properties, including the effect of
    shifts such as f(x + b) and f(x) + c. 1-5 Solving Absolute Value
    Equations and Inequalities, p.42: equations and inequalities in
    forms such as |ax + b| = c and |ax + b| < c, solved graphically
    and algebraically.
  - Unit Two: Exponents, Logarithms and their Applications.
    2-1 Rational exponents, p.52: fractional powers and their laws.
    2-2 Exponential Function and its Application, p.58: growth and
    decay models, including compound interest. 2-3 Exponential
    Equations, p.63. 2-4 The inverse function, p.67: inverse pairs and
    their graphs. 2-5 Logarithmic function and its graph, p.72.
    2-6 Some properties of logarithms, p.78: the log laws applied to
    simplification and equation solving.
  - Unit Three: Limits and continuity.
    3-1 Introduction to limits of functions, p.88: the limit idea
    approached numerically and graphically. 3-2 Finding the limit of
    a function algebraically, p.95: factorisation, rationalisation and
    standard forms. 3-3 Limit of a function at infinity, p.103.
    3-4 Limits of trigonometric functions, p.109. 3-5 Existence of
    limit of a function at a point, p.113: one-sided limits and when
    a limit fails to exist. 3-6 Continuity, p.119: continuity at a
    point and on an interval, and the behaviour that breaks it.
  - Unit Four: Trigonometry.
    4-1 The sine rule, p.130: solving triangles from sides and angles,
    including the ambiguous case in the exercises. 4-2 The cosine
    rule, p.136: solving triangles and proving side/angle relations,
    with life applications (distances, a regular-octagon design) and
    an error-spotting exercise. The book ends with a note sending
    readers to the Ministry website for further general exercises.
- Honest negatives: no sequences or series, no differentiation or
  integration as such (limits only), no probability or statistics, no
  vectors, no matrices, no complex numbers.
- Slips in the book: contents spells the exponential lesson
  "Expnentail Function". The French edition's imprint page is misprinted
  (see R4) and its lesson pagination runs later than the English
  (logarithm function p.73, cosine rule p.140).
- Held-copy defects: none found in the English copy — contents, unit
  openers and last page (printed p.144, end of Unit Four exercises)
  are all present and in order.

    3-1 The 3D orthogonal coordinate system, p.48. 3-2 Vectors in
    space, p.54. 3-3 Vector multiplication, p.63 (dot and cross
    products and their geometric meaning).
    Unit Four: Straight Lines and planes in space. 4-1 The equation
    of a straight line in space, p.80. 4-2 The equation of a plane
    in space, p.90.
  - Second: Differential & Integral Calculus.
    Prerequisites, p.102 (differentiation and integration recap
    from Grade 11).
    Unit One: Differentiation and its Applications. 1-1 Implicit and
    parametric differentiation, p.106. 1-2 Higher derivatives of the
    function, p.112. 1-3 Derivatives of the exponential and
    logarithmic functions, p.116. 1-4 Related time rates, p.126:
    related-rates problems (balloon volume/surface area, a sliding
    ladder) worked in full.
    Unit Two: Behavior of the Function and Curve Sketching. 2-1
    Increasing and decreasing of the functions, p.136. 2-2 Maximum
    and minimum values, p.140. 2-3 Study curves, p.146. 2-4
    Applications on maximum and minimum values, p.156.
    Unit Three: The Definite Integral and its Applications. 3-1
    Integration of the exponential and logarithmic functions, p.164.
    3-2 Methods of integration, p.170: standard integrals table and
    integration by substitution. 3-3 Definite integration, p.180.
    3-4 Application on definite integral, p.187: areas of plane
    regions and volumes of solids of revolution about both axes
    (last page printed p.195).
- Honest negatives: no statistics or probability (separate book);
  no mechanics; no matrices or determinants; no sequences (Grade 11
  material, not revisited as a unit); no conic sections as a unit.
- Slips in the book: contents spells lesson 1-2 "Heigher deivatives".
- Held-copy notes: the English file (199 PDF pages) is 4 pages
  shorter than the Arabic and French files (203 each) for the same
  work — a scan/layout difference, not missing content: the English
  contents run to printed p.195 and the last page is complete
  (volumes-of-revolution exercises 9–24).

### Work 8 — Applied Mathematics (Mechanics), Grade 12, full year (Third Secondary)

- Title as printed: "Applied Mathematics — Mechanics — Third secondary
  — Student Book" (English cover; no term stated — full-year volume).
- Files: Arabic 162 PDF pages, English 163, French 161.
- Authors (English): Mr. Kamal Younis Kabsha, Prof.Dr. Abd-Elshafy
  Fahmi Abada, Prof.Dr. Nabil Tawfik Eldabe, Mr. Osama Gaber
  Abdelhafez, Mr. Magdy Abdelfatah Essafty.
- Edition/year as printed: no deposit number or ISBN is printed
  in the held English or French copies' front matter (cover and
  authors page checked in both; the imprint block is simply absent
  from these scans). The English copy prints no year; the French
  cover prints school year 2025-2026 (CHECKER: visual cover reading
  — the French file's text layer misleadingly carries 2024-2025).
  No deposit/ISBN is assigned here. The book is the Grade 12
  continuation of the Grade 11 Applied series by content and author
  team, but its edition data is unverified.
- Pagination (English, verified): last PDF p.163 = printed p.159;
  printed = PDF − 4.
- Unit map (printed pages, English contents). Two parts:
  First: Statics; Second: Dynamics. Each part opens with an
  unnumbered "prerequisite" section (not given a printed page in
  the contents).
  - First: Statics.
    Unit One: Moments. 1-1 The moment of a force about a point in
    a 2-D coordinate system, p.10: magnitude, direction, moment
    centre and axis, the general theorem of moments, with lever
    and bone/joint applications in the unit opener.
    Unit Two: Parallel coplanar forces. 2-1 The resultant of parallel
    coplanar forces, p.22. 2-2 The equilibrium of a system of
    coplanar force, p.32 (the book's singular "force" is kept here
    as printed).
    Unit Three: Couples. 3-1 Couples, p.42. 3-2 Resultant couple,
    p.50.
  - Second: Dynamics.
    Unit One: Rectilinear Motion. 1-1 Differentiation and
    integrations of the vector functions, p.72: position, velocity
    and acceleration vectors differentiated and integrated with
    respect to time.
    Unit Two: Applications on Newton's Laws of Motion. 2-1 Motion
    of bodies with variable mass or acceleration, p.88. 2-2 Motion
    of connected bodies, p.95. 2-3 Impulse, p.112.
    Unit Three: Work, Energy & Power. 3-1 Work, p.124. 3-2 Energy,
    p.136. 3-3 Power, p.150 (last page printed p.159: power of a
    force, engine power, work done over time intervals, kinetic
    energy and its rate of change).
- Honest negatives: no pure mathematics taught (no new algebra or
  calculus — calculus is used, from Grade 11/12 Pure, but not
  introduced here); no geometry unit; no statistics.
- Slip in the book (contents only): the contents list numbers the
  Power lesson "3-2", duplicating Energy's number; the lesson's own
  running head correctly reads "Power 3-3". The French contents
  number Power correctly as 3-3 (Travail 3-1 p.122, Énergie 3-2
  p.134, Puissance 3-3 p.148); only the English contents duplicate
  3-2 (CHECKER: the draft's "French contents carry the same
  numbering" was wrong). Use 3-3.
- Held-copy notes: the three editions differ by 1–2 PDF pages
  (161–163), normal scan variance; no missing unit was found in
  any edition's contents.

### Work 9 — Statistics, Grade 12, full year (Third Secondary)

- Title as printed: "Statistics — Third form secondary — Student Book"
  (English cover; no term stated — full-year volume).
- Files: Arabic 148 PDF pages, English 146, French 147.
- Authors (English): Mr. Kamal Younis Kabsha, Prof.Dr. Ahmed Kamal
  El-kholy. Revision/preparation: Prof. Shaban Ebrahim Abo Yousif
  and team. Scientific supervision: Ms. Manal Azqoul. Pedagogical
  supervision: Dr. Akram Hassan.
- Edition/year as printed (all three languages, verified):
  first edition 2016/2017, deposit number 8701/2016,
  ISBN 978-977-706-029-5. The English and Arabic held copies are
  2024–2025 prints (introduction page prints "2025-2024"); the
  French held copy is a 2025/2026 print (cover, CHECKER: visual
  reading) — so only the English and Arabic copies are the older
  print year, NOT all three as first drafted.
- Pagination (English, verified): last PDF p.146 = printed p.142;
  printed = PDF − 4. The last printed page is the appendix table of
  areas under the standard normal curve.
- Unit map (printed pages, English contents):
  - Unit one: Correlation and Regression.
    1-1 Correlation, p.4: scatter diagrams, direction and strength
    of relation between two variables, Pearson's correlation
    coefficient, read from graphs and computed with calculator and
    spreadsheet tools. 1-2 Regression, p.16: the simple linear
    regression line of each variable on the other, the regression
    coefficient, estimation from a given line, and research
    applications.
  - Unit two: Advanced Measurements in Statistics.
    2-1 Stem-and-Leaf Data Representation, p.28. 2-2 Quartiles and
    their graphic representation, p.34. 2-3 Semi-interquartile
    range, p.44.
  - Unit three: Probability.
    3-1 Calculating Probability, p.50. 3-2 Conditional Probability,
    p.68. 3-3 Independent Events, p.77.
  - Unit four: Random Variables and Probability Distributions
    (contents short title: "Random Variables and Probability").
    4-1 Discrete random variable, p.86. 4-2 Expectation (mean) and
    Variance of a Discrete Random Variable, p.93. 4-3 Geometric and
    Binomial Distributions, p.100. 4-4 Density function of the
    continuous random variable, p.108.
  - Unit five: Normal distribution.
    5-1 Normal distribution, p.116. 5-2 Some practical applications
    of the normal distribution, p.130. 5-3 Estimation and Confidence
    Intervals, p.136. Appendix: table of areas under the standard
    normal distribution curve, printed p.142.
- Honest negatives: no economics content at all (that belongs to the
  owed Economics+Statistics file, if it exists as a separate book);
  no mean/standard-deviation unit as a main topic (dispersion is
  handled through quartiles and the semi-interquartile range);
  no hypothesis testing beyond confidence intervals; no
  combinatorics taught here (counting lives in Grade 11 Pure).
- Held-copy notes: no defects found; the English text layer is
  complete and matches the visual contents.

- R5 — Arabic General Term 1 page count: the file's 191 PDF pages

Headline tests:

- French Term 2 genuinely absent: CONFIRMED. File inventory: the
  only French files are G11 Term 1 in all three branches plus the
  three G12 full-year books. Covers read: Pure FR "Premier
  Semestre" (with the misprinted Term 2 imprint — genuine Term 1
  file), Applied FR "Premier semestre", General FR "Premier
  Semestre". The Arabic General T1 second copy repeats Term 1.
- Grade 12 single full-year volumes: CONFIRMED. W7/W8/W9 English
  covers state no term; contents span the whole year (W7 binomial
  theorem → definite integrals; W8 statics → work/energy/power;
  W9 correlation → confidence intervals). No term-split G12 files
  exist in the set.
- Applied G12 solid geometry: NOT CONFIRMED — the premise does not
  hold for Grade 12, and the draft was right. G12 Applied contents
  contain no geometry unit at all; the only "sphere" hits are
  mechanics bodies (a pendulum bob, PDF p.153; a metal ball in a
  dusty atmosphere, French PDF p.94), not solid-geometry teaching.

  negative ("no geometry unit") was needed — it is correct.

Reader's honest limits: CONFIRMED as a method, with contents-derived
details as the weak point — not lesson descriptions. Every
lesson-interior page opened (5+ per image-only work, across units)
matched the draft's description of that lesson, including worked
examples, contexts and exercise content. All six corrections above
came from places where the reader had trusted a contents page or
generalised across editions: one contents misprint copied verbatim
(W2 3-4), one endpoint read short (D1 copy 2), and three
cross-edition over-generalisations (W9 French print year, W8 French
contents numbering, W8 French cover year). Non-English pagination
was verified independently and does differ: French end offsets run
PDF−4 (Pure T1, General T1, Statistics) to PDF−6 (Applied T1,
Pure G12); Arabic General T2 is PDF−4 like English, while Arabic
Pure T2 runs several pages earlier than English (contents 1-2 at
p.9 vs 13). The draft's warning not to assume the English offset
for other editions is borne out.

No files were committed to any repo. The draft was corrected in
place only.
